size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab (owned planes plus ghost planes) with the same\n// deterministic pattern the serial code uses on the global grid.\nvoid initializeLocalGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                         const size_t nz, const size_t gz0, const size_t nplanes) {\n    const size_t planeSize = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t lz = 0; lz < nplanes; ++lz) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t gz = gz0 + lz;\n            if (gz >= nz) continue;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t gidx = idx3(x, y, gz, nx, ny);\n                grid[lz * planeSize + y * nx + x] = (gidx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil update for one output point at local plane lz (global plane gz).\n// Global boundary points are copied from the input, matching the serial code.\n__device__ inline void stencilPoint(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out,\n                                    const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t lz,\n                                    const size_t gz, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                                    const size_t gnz) {\n    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, lz, nx, ny);\n    if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1 || gz == 0 || gz == gnz - 1) {\n        out[idx] = in[idx];\n        return;\n    }\n    const Real center = in[idx];\n    const Real left   = in[idx - 1];\n    const Real right  = in[idx + 1];\n    const Real front  = in[idx - nx];\n    const Real back   = in[idx + nx];\n    const Real bottom = in[idx - nx * ny];\n    const Real top    = in[idx + nx * ny];\n    out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n}\n\n// Updates owned planes lz in [2, nzl-1] (those that do not read ghost planes).\n__global__ void stencilInterior(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nzl,\n                                const size_t gz0, const size_t gnz) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t lz = blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + 2;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || lz > nzl - 1) return;\n    stencilPoint(in, out, x, y, lz, gz0 + lz - 1, nx, ny, gnz);\n}\n\n// Updates the first (lz = 1) and last (lz = nzl) owned planes, which depend on\n// the ghost planes received from the MPI neighbors.\n__global__ void stencilBoundary(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nzl,\n                                const size_t gz0, const size_t gnz) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n    const size_t lz = (blockIdx.z == {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011Cez1keXJNfPkUmZ5mgdCC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The stencil3d benchmark is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program, verified bit-identical to the original serial code and scaling across both GPUs.\n\n**Design** (all in the existing `stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt`, executable still `stencil3d`):\n\n- **MPI**: 1D slab decomposition along Z. Each rank owns a contiguous set of Z-planes plus two ghost planes, picks a GPU round-robin by node-local rank (via `MPI_Comm_split_type`), and exchanges one halo plane per neighbor per iteration through pinned host staging buffers with `MPI_Sendrecv`. Ranks in excess of the plane count (e.g. 5 ranks on a 3-plane grid) sit out the compute but still join the collectives.\n- **CUDA**: the 7-point stencil runs entirely on the GPU with double buffering in device memory (no host round-trip between iterations). Two kernels split the work: an interior kernel over planes that don't touch ghosts, launched on one stream so it **overlaps with the MPI halo exchange**, and a boundary-plane kernel that runs after the ghosts arrive on a second stream. Global-boundary copy semantics of the original code are preserved inside the kernels.\n- **OpenMP**: parallelizes host-side grid initialization (done per-rank with global indices, so no initial broadcast is needed) and the validation min/max/NaN scan on rank 0.\n- **CMake**: enables the CUDA language (preferring `/usr/local/cuda`'s nvcc 12.6, since the 12.0 on PATH rejects the system gcc 13), builds for the native GPU architecture, and passes `-O3 -march=native -fopenmp` to the host compiler through nvcc; MPI includes/libs are added directly so nvcc never sees host-only flags like `-pthread`.\n\n**Verification**: I rebuilt the original serial code in `/tmp` as a reference and compared the `-r` results output (sum/min/max/hash/samples) across 4 grid shapes × 1/2/3/5 ranks — all 16 combinations match exactly, and `-v` validation passes.\n\n**Performance** on the two RTX 3090s, 512³ grid × 40 iterations: 20,023 MCellUpdates/s on 1 rank/GPU and 35,139 MCellUpdates/s on 2 ranks (1.75× multi-GPU scaling); the original serial code measures roughly 70 MCellUpdates/s on the same grid, so this is about a 500× speedup at 2 GPUs."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":515,"cache_read_input_tokens":39626,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":515},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2a7304be-a38f-4c9b-a09f-359ee78e22d6","uuid":"ceddf3ee-a627-4a79-9d27-1aeb0e36ae40","timestamp":"2026-09-12T18:24:57.421Z","request_id":"req_011Cez1kdJ867C2H3gNg7uhz"}
